**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/atan2.html

**text**　# Name: ATan2_Ex_02.py
# Description: Calculates the inverse tangent of pixels based
#              on (y, x) values from two rasters
# Requirements: Image Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.ia import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/iapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster1 = "degs"
inRaster2 = "negs"

# Run ATan2
outATan2 = ATan2(inRaster1, inRaster2)

# Save the output
outATan2.save("C:/iapyexamples/output/outatan2.tif")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/atan2.html#return-value

## ATan2 (影像分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱

**id**　97d7d1ef413d2f9a7f-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/atan2.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 ATan2，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網格或常量值 1 [in_raster_or_constant1]、輸入網格或常量值 2 [in_raster_or_constant2]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

A number can be used as an input for this parameter, provided a raster is specified for the other parameter. To specify a number for both inputs, the cell size and extent must first be set in the environment."}]

**signatures**　
- ATan2(in_raster_or_constant1, in_raster_or_constant2)

## 002953: 無法處理幾何。 · 解決方案

**id**　97d9346dadf591d343-3

**english**　002953: Unable to process the geometry.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 2901-3000

**kind**　有序步驟

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02951-02975-002953.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02951-02975-002953.html

**steps**　
1. 使用 檢查幾何 工具可生成參考資料中幾何問題的報告。
2. 使用 修復幾何 工具可修復 檢查幾何 工具標識的幾何問題。 注意： 在運行該工具之前，請對參考資料進行備份，因為 修復幾何 工具會修改輸入圖徵。
3. 可以使用經過修復的參考資料來構建定位器。
4. 在修復點或面參考資料中的無效幾何后，如果錯誤仍然存在，請刪除標識的圖徵并再次創建定位器。 如果圖徵為折線，請使用 增密化 工具（ 偏移 作為 增密方法 參數值）來簡化可能存在于參考資料中的曲線，例如死巷。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02951-02975-002953.html#solution

## 鄰域匯總統計資料 (空間統計工具) · 匯總

**id**　97e4fec491f6875413-1

**english**　Neighborhood Summary Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 度量地理分布工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html

**text**　使用每個圖徵周圍的局部鄰域來計算一個或多個數值欄位的匯總統計資料。 局部統計資料包括平均值、中位數、標準差、四分位距、偏度和分位數不平衡。 所有統計資料都可以使用核來進行地理加權，以對更靠近焦點圖徵的相鄰圖徵產生更大影響。 可以使用各種鄰域類型，其中包括距離范圍、鄰居數、面鄰接、Delaunay 三角測量和空間權重矩陣文件 (.swm)。 還會針對與每個圖徵的相鄰圖徵的距離來計算匯總統計資料。
了解有關“鄰域匯總統計資料”工作原理的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html#summary

## 鄰域匯總統計資料 (空間統計工具) · 用法

**id**　97e4fec491f6875413-2

**english**　Neighborhood Summary Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 度量地理分布工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html

**text**　除了提供的任何分析欄位之外，還會針對每個圖徵計算基于到相鄰圖徵距離的統計資料。 例如，可以計算到每個相鄰圖徵距離的平均值和標準差，以查看相鄰圖徵通常距離焦點圖徵較近或較遠。 到相鄰圖徵距離的統計資料將不會在其計算中使用焦點圖徵，因為該距離始終為零。 此外，將不會對這些統計資料進行地理加權，因為其對于基于上述相同距離的權重距離值沒有意義。
當為鄰域類型參數指定 Delaunay 三角測量選項時，輸出圖徵類名為 Voronoi Map。
如果輸入圖徵是面，則面之間的所有距離均由面質心之間的距離定義。
由于不應該通過質心到質心的距離對基于面鄰接或 Delaunay 三角測量的鄰域進行加權，因此僅當鄰域類型參數指定為鄰居數或距離范圍時，才能對局部統計資料進行地理加權。 如果為鄰域類型參數指定了從文件獲取空間權重，則空間權重文件中定義的權重將自動用于地理加權。
如果將“輸出座標系”環境設置為具有緯度和經度座標的地理座標系，則將使用弦距離來計算所有距離。 否則，將使用直線（歐氏）距離來計算所有距離。
如果您選擇使用忽略計算中的空值參數來忽略空值，則在搜索相鄰圖徵后，將過濾掉分析欄位中具有空值的相鄰圖徵。 這可能會導致相鄰圖徵的數目少于預期，并且如果提供多個分析欄位，則某些欄位所使用的相鄰圖徵的數目可能會與其他欄位不同。 當為鄰域類型參數指定鄰居數選項時，這一點尤其明顯。
分析欄位的局部均值通常被稱為欄位的空間滯后。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html#usage

- String

## 鄰域匯總統計資料 (空間統計工具) · 參數

**id**　97e4fec491f6875413-10

**english**　Neighborhood Summary Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 度量地理分布工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html

**parameter**　neighborhood_type

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 鄰域類型 （可選）
- 指定將哪些圖徵作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 要計算局部統計資料，必須為每個輸入圖徵標識相鄰圖徵，并且這些相鄰圖徵將用于計算每個圖徵的局部統計資料。 僅當具有 Desktop Advanced 許可時，Delaunay 三角測量選項才可用。 距離范圍—每個圖徵指定臨界距離內的圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 鄰居數—最近圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 僅鄰接邊—共享邊的面圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 鄰接邊拐角—共享邊或拐角的面圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 這是面圖徵的默認選項。 Delaunay 三角測量—其 Delaunay 三角測量共享邊的圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 這是點圖徵的默認選項。 通過文件獲取空間權重—將由指定空間權重文件定義相鄰圖徵和權重。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html#parameters

- neighborhood_type （可選）
- 指定將哪些圖徵作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 要計算局部統計資料，必須為每個輸入圖徵標識相鄰圖徵，并且這些相鄰圖徵將用于計算每個圖徵的局部統計資料。 僅當具有 Desktop Advanced 許可時，Delaunay 三角測量選項才可用。 DISTANCE_BAND—每個圖徵指定臨界距離內的圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 NUMBER_OF_NEIGHBORS—最近圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 CONTIGUITY_EDGES_ONLY—共享邊的面圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 CONTIGUITY_EDGES_CORNERS—共享邊或拐角的面圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 這是面圖徵的默認選項。 DELAUNAY_TRIANGULATION—其 Delaunay 三角測量共享邊的圖徵將作為相鄰圖徵包含在內。 這是點圖徵的默認選項。 GET_SPATIAL_WEIGHTS_FROM_FILE—將由指定空間權重文件定義相鄰圖徵和權重。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html#parameters

**text**　# Calculate a focal mean for the population and income of US cities.

import arcpy

# Set the current workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = r"c:\data\project_data.gdb"

# Calculate the local mean of POP2020 and MedIncome2020 fields
# using 8 nearest neighbors.

arcpy.stats.NeighborhoodSummaryStatistics("USCities", "USCities_Mean",
      "POP2020;MedIncome2020", "MEAN", "EXCLUDE_FOCAL", "IGNORE_NULLS",
      "NUMBER_OF_NEIGHBORS", None, 8, None, "GAUSSIAN", "50 Miles")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 鄰域匯總統計資料 (空間統計工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 度量地理分布工具集

**english**　Neighborhood Summary Statistics

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html

**id**　97e4fec491f6875413-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Neighborhood Summary Statistics，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [in_features]、輸出圖徵 [output_features]、分析欄位 （可選） [analysis_fields]、局部匯總統計資料 （可選） [local_summary_statistic]、在計算中包括焦點圖徵 （可選） [include_focal_feature]、忽略計算中的空值 （可選） [ignore_nulls]、鄰域類型 （可選） [neighborhood_type]、距離范圍 （可選） [distance_band]、鄰居數 （可選） [number_of_neighbors]、權重矩陣文件 （可選） [weights_matrix_file]、局部權重方案 （可選） [local_weighting_scheme]、核帶寬 （可選） [kernel_bandwidth]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/neighborhood-summary-statistics.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_features", "label_zh": "輸入圖徵", "label_en": "Input Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The point or polygon features that will be used to calculate the local statistics."}, {"parameter": "output_features", "label_zh": "輸出圖徵", "label_en": "Output Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output feature class containing the local statistics as fields. Each statistic of each analysis field will be stored as an individual field."}, {"parameter": "analysis_fields", "label_zh": "分析欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Analysis Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "One or more fields that will be used to calculate local statistics. If no analysis fields are provided, only local statistics based on distances to neighbors will be calculated."}, {"parameter": "local_summary_statistic", "label_zh": "局部匯總統計資料 （可選）", "label_en": "Local Summary Statistic (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the local summary statistic that will be calculated for each analysis field. All—All local statistics will be calculated. This is the default. Mean—The local mean (average) will be calculated. Median—The local median will be calculated. Standard deviation—The local standard deviation will be calculated. Interquartile range—The local interquartile range will be calculated. Skewness—The local skewness will be calculated. Quantile imbalance—The local quantile imbalance will be calculated."}, {"parameter": "include_focal_feature", "label_zh": "在計算中包括焦點圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Include Focal Feature in Calculations (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the focal feature will be included when calculating local statistics for each feature. Checked—The focal feature and all of its neighbors will be included when calculating local statistics. This is the default. Unchecked—The focal feature will not be included when calculating local statistics. Only neighbors of the feature will be included."}, {"parameter": "ignore_nulls", "label_zh": "忽略計算中的空值 （可選）", "label_en": "Ignore Null Values in Calculations (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether null values in the analysis fields will be included or ignored in the calculations. Checked—Null values will be ignored in the local calculations. Unchecked—Null values will be included in the local calculations."}, {"parameter": "neighborhood_type", "label_zh": "鄰域類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Neighborhood Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies which features will be included as neighbors. To calculate local statistics, neighboring features must be identified for each input feature, and these neighbors are used to calculate the local statistics for each feature. The Delaunay triangulation option is only available with a Desktop Advanced license. Distance band—Features within a specified critical distance of each feature will be included as neighbors. Number of neighbors—The closest features will be included as neighbors. Contiguity edges only—Polygon features that share an edge will be included as neighbors. Contiguity edges corners—Polygon features that share an edge or a corner will be included as neighbors. This is the default for polygon features. Delaunay triangulation—Features whose Delaunay triangulation share an edge will be included as neighbors. This is the default for point features. Get spatial weights from file—Neighbors and weights will be defined by a specified spatial weights file."}, {"parameter": "distance_band", "label_zh": "距離范圍 （可選）", "label_en": "Distance Band (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "All features within this distance will be included as neighbors. If no value is provided, one will be estimated during processing and included as a geoprocessing message. If the specified distance results in more than 1,000 neighbors, only the closest 1,000 features will be included as neighbors."}, {"parameter": "number_of_neighbors", "label_zh": "鄰居數 （可選）", "label_en": "Number of Neighbors (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of neighbors that will be included for each local calculation. The number does not include the focal feature. If the focal feature is included in calculations, one additional neighbor will be used. The default is 8."}, {"parameter": "weights_matrix_file", "label_zh": "權重矩陣文件 （可選）", "label_en": "Weights Matrix File (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The path and file name of the spatial weights matrix file that defines spatial, and potentially temporal, relationships among features."}, {"parameter": "local_weighting_scheme", "label_zh": "局部權重方案 （可選）", "label_en": "Local Weighting Scheme (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the weighting scheme that will be applied to neighbors when calculating local statistics. Unweighted—Neighbors will not be weighted. This is the default. Bisquare—Neighbors will be weighted using a bisquare kernel scheme. Gaussian—Neighbors will be weighted using a Gaussian kernel scheme."}, {"parameter": "kernel_bandwidth", "label_zh": "核帶寬 （可選）", "label_en": "Kernel Bandwidth (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The bandwidth of the bisquare or Gaussian local weighting schemes. If no value is provided, one will be estimated during processing and included as a geoprocessing message."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.stats.NeighborhoodSummaryStatistics(in_features, output_features, {analysis_fields}, {local_summary_statistic}, {include_focal_feature}, {ignore_nulls}, {neighborhood_type}, {distance_band}, {number_of_neighbors}, {weights_matrix_file}, {local_weighting_scheme}, {kernel_bandwidth})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_features、output_features、analysis_fields、local_summary_statistic、include_focal_feature、ignore_nulls、neighborhood_type、distance_band、number_of_neighbors、weights_matrix_file、local_weighting_scheme、kernel_bandwidth。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## Generate Elevation Guide Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 匯總

**id**　97f186cffddf78d189-1

**english**　Generate Elevation Guide Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html

**text**　Creates data required for an elevation guide diagram surround element as required by various supported map product specifications. This tool uses existing banding and thinning parameters to generate output elevation band features, spot height features, and hydrology features.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#summary

## Generate Elevation Guide Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　97f186cffddf78d189-2

**english**　Generate Elevation Guide Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html

**text**　The tool will create, at a minimum, the following feature classes in the target feature dataset: EGB_COAST_L EGB_BANDS_A EGB_BANDS_L EGB_HYDRO_A EGB_HYDRO_L EGB_SPOTS_P If input hydrology line or input hydrology area features are provided, the tool will create the EGB_HYDRO_A and EGB_HYDRO_L feature classes as required with a selection of thinned hydrology features as specified by the Minimum Hydro Length and Minimum Hydro Area parameters. If input spot height features are provided, the tool will also create the EGB_SPOTS_P feature class and will populate this feature class with a selection of thinned spot heights based on the provided search distance. These feature classes will manage elevation bands and coastlines in a common format that can be used by product templates to preconfigure layers and required symbology for the elevation guide diagram.
The symbology for the elevation band features is intended to be separated between band areas and lines to better support elevation band line masking requirements for overlapping spot height annotation.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#usage

## Generate Elevation Guide Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　97f186cffddf78d189-3

**english**　Generate Elevation Guide Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html

**text**　The coastline features will only be generated if a set of hydrology exclusion features are available when processing the elevation bands. Without hydrology exclusion features, no hydrology features will be created by the banding tools to identify the coastline, as the area of interest will be assumed to be 100 percent land.
If more than one input raster is used, all input rasters must have the same cell size, band count, and pixel type. All input rasters should also use the same elevation units (meters or feet).
When the area of interest extent is not completely within the raster extent, incorrect bands may be generated.
If a value is not specified for the Number Of Elevation Bands parameter, the tool will create the number of bands that the map product specification describes. For example, the Topographic Data Store (TDS) Topographic Map (TM) specification section 3.3.6.3 about elevation guide diagrams uses zero elevation bands if the range of elevation is anywhere from 0 to 100 meters, three bands for 100 to 600 meters, and four bands for 600 to more meters.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#usage

**parameter**　contour_interval

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Contour Interval （可選）
- Specifies the contour interval that will be used to determine the closest available contour when calculating the elevation band area. Elevation bands are created with their limits aligned to the specified contour interval, except low and high values, which will represent their actual calculated values. 10—A contour interval of 10 will be used. 20—A contour interval of 20 will be used. This is the default. 40—A contour interval of 40 will be used. 80—A contour interval of 80 will be used.
- Long

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#parameters

**id**　97f186cffddf78d189-20

**english**　Generate Elevation Guide Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html

**parameter**　hydroline_minspacing

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Minimum Hydro Line Spacing （可選）
- The shortest distance between hydrographic segments that will display at the output scale. If the spacing between two parallel trending features is smaller than this value, one of the features will be hidden. This parameter defines the density of the resulting thinned hydrography. It should correspond to the distance between two parallel trending features that is visually significant to include at the final scale. When the density of features is too high (that is, the features are too closely spaced), at least one feature will be hidden. This can result in important features or features longer than the Minimum Hydro Line Length value being hidden.
- Linear Unit

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#parameters

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- contour_interval （可選）
- Specifies the contour interval that will be used to determine the closest available contour when calculating the elevation band area. Elevation bands are created with their limits aligned to the specified contour interval, except low and high values, which will represent their actual calculated values. 10—A contour interval of 10 will be used. 20—A contour interval of 20 will be used. This is the default. 40—A contour interval of 40 will be used. 80—A contour interval of 80 will be used.
- Long

**source_language**　zh-cn

**parameter**　bands_minarea

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- bands_minarea （可選）
- The minimum area for output polygons. Features smaller than this value will be removed. The default is 0.00016 square decimal degrees. 注: If you are creating an output dataset with a projected coordinate system, ensure that this value reflects the square units of that coordinate system—for example, square meters for a UTM dataset. Otherwise, the default value may result in an empty output dataset.
- Double

**parameter**　hydroline_minspacing

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- hydroline_minspacing （可選）
- The shortest distance between hydrographic segments that will display at the output scale. If the spacing between two parallel trending features is smaller than this value, one of the features will be hidden. This parameter defines the density of the resulting thinned hydrography. It should correspond to the distance between two parallel trending features that is visually significant to include at the final scale. When the density of features is too high (that is, the features are too closely spaced), at least one feature will be hidden. This can result in important features or features longer than the hydroline_minlength value being hidden.
- Linear Unit

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#derived-output

**english**　Generate Elevation Guide Features

**text**　GenerateElevationGuideFeatures example (stand-alone script)
This Python stand-alone sample creates a dataset in a file geodatabase as well as the Elevation Guide Data features in the dataset.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#derived-output

## Generate Elevation Guide Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　97f186cffddf78d189-46

**english**　Generate Elevation Guide Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html

**text**　# Name: GenerateElevationGuideFeatures_sample.py
# Description: Creating a dataset in a file geodatabase, and creating the Elevation Guide Data features in that dataset

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Setting the environment
arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = True

# Setting Local Variables
input_database = r'C:\ProProjects\Random\ElevationGuideData.gdb'
aoi = r'C:\Data\MapIndex.gdb\MapIndex\TLM50_Index'
raster = r'C:\Data\Rasters\Monterey.dt2'
hydro_exclusion = r'C:\Data\MGCP_TRD_4_4.gdb\MGCP\CoastA'
spot_features = r'C:\Data\MGCP_TRD_4_4.gdb\MGCP_Delta\ElevP'
hydro_lines = r'C:\Data\MGCP_TRD_4_4.gdb\MGCP\WatrcrsL'
hydro_areas = r'C:\Data\MGCP_TRD_4_4.gdb\MGCP\WatrcrsA'

# Getting spatial reference from spot features database
desc = arcpy.Describe(spot_features)
sr = desc.spatialReference

# Creating a dataset in the input database
input_dataset = arcpy.management.CreateFeatureDataset(input_database, 'CARTO', sr).getOutput(0)

# Making a feature layer from the Area of Interest features, and selecting a single feature based on the NRN field
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(aoi, 'AOI_Index')
arcpy.management.SelectLayerByAttribute('AOI_Index', 'NEW_SELECTION', "NRN = 'V795X16573'")

# Creating feature layers for spot and hydro features
arcpy.management. MakeFeatureLayer(hydro_exclusion, 'CoastA')
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(spot_features, 'ElevP')
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(hydro_lines, 'WatrcrsL')
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(hydro_areas, 'WatrcrsA')

# Calling the Generate Elevation Guide Features tool to create the elevation guide data over Monterey
arcpy.topographic.GenerateElevationGuideFeatures(input_dataset, 'AOI_Index', raster, 'CoastA', 'ElevP', 'WatrcrsL',
                                                 'WatrcrsA', '20', '#', '#', '#', 'zvh', '600 METERS',
                                                 '50 METERS', '50 METERS', '50 METERS', '50 METERS', '40000 SquareMeters')

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#derived-output

## Generate Elevation Guide Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**english**　Generate Elevation Guide Features

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html

**id**　97f186cffddf78d189-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-guide-features.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate Elevation Guide Features，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Feature Dataset [in_feature_dataset]、Area Of Interest [area_of_interest]、Input Rasters [in_rasters]、Hydro Exclusion Features （可選） [hydro_exclusion_features]、Spot Height Features （可選） [spot_height_features]、Hydro Line Features （可選） [hydro_line_features]、Hydro Area Features （可選） [hydro_area_features]、Contour Interval （可選） [contour_interval]、Minimum Feature Area （可選） [bands_minarea]、Smoothing Tolerance （可選） [smooth_tolerance]、Number Of Elevation Bands （可選） [number_of_bands]、Height Field （可選） [height_field]、Search Distance （可選） [search_distance]、Minimum Hydro Line Length （可選） [hydroline_minlength]、Minimum Hydro Line Spacing （可選） [hydroline_minspacing]、Minimum Hydro Area Length （可選） [hydroarea_minlength]、Minimum Hydro Area Width （可選） [hydroarea_minwidth]、Minimum Island Area （可選） [min_island_area]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

Data created for the elevation guide box is maintained in these feature classes in this feature dataset."}, {"parameter": "area_of_interest", "label_zh": "Area Of Interest", "label_en": "Area Of Interest", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "A feature layer with a single selected feature that will define the processing extent for banding operations and a clipping extent for spot heights, and input hydrology areas and lines."}, {"parameter": "in_rasters", "label_zh": "Input Rasters", "label_en": "Input Rasters", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Mosaic Layer", "description_en": "One or more rasters that will be used to create elevation bands and supply elevation values to the created features."}, {"parameter": "hydro_exclusion_features", "label_zh": "Hydro Exclusion Features （可選）", "label_en": "Hydro Exclusion Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "A feature layer that defines a large water body area that will be excluded from the elevation band area computation."}, {"parameter": "spot_height_features", "label_zh": "Spot Height Features （可選）", "label_en": "Spot Height Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "A feature layer or class that contains spot heights."}, {"parameter": "hydro_line_features", "label_zh": "Hydro Line Features （可選）", "label_en": "Hydro Line Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "Hydrology line features that will be used to generate the output of a thinned hydrology dataset. Only the output features will be generalized through this thinning process."}, {"parameter": "hydro_area_features", "label_zh": "Hydro Area Features （可選）", "label_en": "Hydro Area Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "Hydrology area features that will be used to generate the thinned hydrology dataset. Only the output features will be generalized through this thinning process."}, {"parameter": "contour_interval", "label_zh": "Contour Interval （可選）", "label_en": "Contour Interval (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "Specifies the contour interval that will be used to determine the closest available contour when calculating the elevation band area. Elevation bands are created with their limits aligned to the specified contour interval, except low and high values, which will represent their actual calculated values. 10—A contour interval of 10 will be used. 20—A contour interval of 20 will be used. This is the default. 40—A contour interval of 40 will be used. 80—A contour interval of 80 will be used."}, {"parameter": "bands_minarea", "label_zh": "Minimum Feature Area （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Feature Area (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The minimum area for output polygons. Features smaller than this value will be removed. The default is 0.00016 square decimal degrees. Note: If you are creating an output dataset with a projected coordinate system, ensure that this value reflects the square units of that coordinate system—for example, square meters for a UTM dataset. Otherwise, the default value may result in an empty output dataset."}, {"parameter": "smooth_tolerance", "label_zh": "Smoothing Tolerance （可選）", "label_en": "Smoothing Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The tolerance that will be used by the smoothing algorithm. The larger the value, the more generalized the output band features. The default is 0.002 decimal degrees."}, {"parameter": "number_of_bands", "label_zh": "Number Of Elevation Bands （可選）", "label_en": "Number Of Elevation Bands (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "Specifies the number of elevation bands that will be generated. 1—One elevation band will be generated. 2—Two elevation bands will be generated. 3—Three elevation bands will be generated. 4—Four elevation bands will be generated."}, {"parameter": "height_field", "label_zh": "Height Field （可選）", "label_en": "Height Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field that identifies the elevation values of the spot height features. These values will be evaluated during the thinning process."}, {"parameter": "search_distance", "label_zh": "Search Distance （可選）", "label_en": "Search Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The minimum distance between spot heights. The default is 0 meters."}, {"parameter": "hydroline_minlength", "label_zh": "Minimum Hydro Line Length （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Hydro Line Length (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The minimum length that will be used to eliminate hydrology features. Hydrology features that have a length less than this value will be thinned. This value is used when generalizing input hydro lines and areas."}, {"parameter": "hydroline_minspacing", "label_zh": "Minimum Hydro Line Spacing （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Hydro Line Spacing (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The shortest distance between hydrographic segments that will display at the output scale. If the spacing between two parallel trending features is smaller than this value, one of the features will be hidden. This parameter defines the density of the resulting thinned hydrography. It should correspond to the distance between two parallel trending features that is visually significant to include at the final scale. When the density of features is too high (that is, the features are too closely spaced), at least one feature will be hidden. This can result in important features or features longer than the Minimum Hydro Line Length value being hidden."}, {"parameter": "hydroarea_minlength", "label_zh": "Minimum Hydro Area Length （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Hydro Area Length (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The length that will be used to split and classify hydrographic polygons as short or long. Polygons will be split at any location where the edge-to-edge distance is equal to this value."}, {"parameter": "hydroarea_minwidth", "label_zh": "Minimum Hydro Area Width （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Hydro Area Width (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The width that will be used to split and classify hydrographic polygons as narrow or wide. Polygons will be split at any location where the edge-to-edge distance is equal to this value."}, {"parameter": "min_island_area", "label_zh": "Minimum Island Area （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Island Area (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Areal Unit", "description_en": "The minimum area necessary for an island or hole to be included in the resulting features."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.topographic.GenerateElevationGuideFeatures(in_feature_dataset, area_of_interest, in_rasters, {hydro_exclusion_features}, {spot_height_features}, {hydro_line_features}, {hydro_area_features}, {contour_interval}, {bands_minarea}, {smooth_tolerance}, {number_of_bands}, {height_field}, {search_distance}, {hydroline_minlength}, {hydroline_minspacing}, {hydroarea_minlength}, {hydroarea_minwidth}, {min_island_area})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_feature_dataset、area_of_interest、in_rasters、hydro_exclusion_features、spot_height_features、hydro_line_features、hydro_area_features、contour_interval、bands_minarea、smooth_tolerance、number_of_bands、height_field、search_distance、hydroline_minlength、hydroline_minspacing、hydroarea_minlength、hydroarea_minwidth、min_island_area。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 描述 NetCDF 文件 (多維工具) · 用法

**id**　97fda919e7a78e1623-2

**english**　Describe NetCDF File

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 多維工具箱
- NetCDF 工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/multidimension/describe-netcdf-file.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/multidimension/describe-netcdf-file.html

**text**　該工具支持帶有 .nc 或 .nc4 擴展名的 netCDF-3 和 netCDF-4 格式。
該工具生成地理處理消息和可選的 Markdown 文件以供輸出。 此工具的地理處理消息描述了輸入 netCDF 文件的特征和內容。 這些消息包含如下關于 netCDF 文件的信息： 輸入 netCDF 文件路徑。 “維度”表描述了變量的形狀。 其中包含三列：維度、大小和變量。 每一行指定了維度名稱、維度大小及相關變量。 “變量”表列出了變量信息。 其中包含四列：變量、資料類型、維度和屬性。 每個變量可以與一個或多個維度相關聯，并能存儲不同類型的資料（如整型、浮點型或字符）。 屬性提供了關于變量中資料的上下文和細節，包括計量單位、描述及缺失值的信息。 “全局屬性”表記錄了元資料。 其中包含兩列：屬性名稱和屬性值。 該表用于存儲常規信息，例如資料集的來源、約定、歷史記錄和其他詳細信息。
如果為輸出描述文件（Python 中的 out_file）參數提供值，工具將生成一個表格，包含地理處理消息的內容。 輸出描述文件可另存為 .txt、.cdl 或 .md 文件。 如果未指定文件擴展名，輸出文件將默認保存為 .txt 文件。
該工具不支持讀取由“時空特征挖掘”工具箱生成的 netCDF 文件。 使用描述時空立方體工具，該工具提供了增強功能來描述此類型文件的內容。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/multidimension/describe-netcdf-file.html#usage

## 描述 NetCDF 文件 (多維工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　97fda919e7a78e1623-12

**english**　Describe NetCDF File

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 多維工具箱
- NetCDF 工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/multidimension/describe-netcdf-file.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/multidimension/describe-netcdf-file.html

**text**　# Name: DescribeNetCDFFile.py
# Description: Describe a NetCDF dataset.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data"

# Set a variable
in_netCDF_file = r"C:\example\airtemp.nc"
out_file = r"C:\example\summary.md"

# Run DescribeNetCDFFile
arcpy.md.DescribeNetCDFFile(in_netCDF_file, out_file)

## 描述 NetCDF 文件 (多維工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 多維工具箱
- NetCDF 工具集

**english**　Describe NetCDF File

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/multidimension/describe-netcdf-file.html

**id**　97fda919e7a78e1623-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/multidimension/describe-netcdf-file.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Describe NetCDF File，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入 netCDF 文件 [in_netCDF_file]、輸出描述文件 （可選） [out_file]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 批量導入資料 (AllSource 工具) · 用法

**id**　9801b82e9458117cbc-2

**english**　Batch Import Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- AllSource 工具箱
- 轉換工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/intelligence/batch-import-data.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/intelligence/batch-import-data.html

**text**　如果輸入資料值為文件夾，則將遞歸搜索該文件夾以查找 KML (.kml) 和 KMZ (.kmz) 文件、shapefile (.shp)、Excel 工作簿（.xlsx 和 .xls）、表格文本文件（.csv、.txt 和 .tab）、GeoJSON 文件 (.geojson) 以及 GPX 文件 (.gpx)。 過濾器參數可用于限制從文件夾導入哪些文件。
.kml 或 .kmz 文件中包含的網格或地面疊加層將轉換為目標地理資料庫參數中的鑲嵌資料集。 鑲嵌資料集引用的源網格以其原生格式存儲在以鑲嵌資料集命名的子文件夾中。 其位置與目標地理資料庫相同。 這些文件以目標地理資料庫值命名，并在名稱后追加 _Rasters。 例如，如果您的目標地理資料庫值為 C:\Data\OperationalData.gdb，則會在 C:\Data 下創建一個名為 OperationalData_Rasters 的文件夾。
如果要將文件導入到企業級地理資料庫，請確保資料庫連接文件 (.sde) 被指定為目標地理資料庫參數中的 UNC 路徑，且企業級地理資料庫可訪問該路徑。
通常支持開放地理空間聯盟 KML標準中 KMZ 2.2 及以下版本的 KML 和 KMZ 輸入。 不支持使用地址選項卡（按地理編碼方式）的點位置。 源 KML 內需要有效的經度和緯度位置。
擴展名為 .csv 或 .txt 的表格文本文件的標準分隔符是逗號，擴展名為 .tab 的表格文本文件的標準分隔符是制表符。 要使用包含非標準分隔符的輸入表，必須使用 schema.ini 文件指定分隔符。 有關詳細信息，請參閱 Microsoft 文檔中的 Schema.ini 文件（文本文件驅動程序）。
對于輸入 Excel 工作表和表格文本文件，僅支持點幾何。
GeoJSON 規范支持多種幾何類型。 將使用命名約定 SourceGeoJSONFile_GeometryType 為輸入 .geojson 文件中的每個幾何類型創建單獨的圖徵類。 例如，如果 .geojson 文件命名為 World Airports.geojson，且包含點、多點、折線和面幾何類型，則圖徵類將命名為 World Airports_Points、World Airports_Multipoint、World Airports_Polylines 和 World Airports_Polygons。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/intelligence/batch-import-data.html#usage

## 批量導入資料 (AllSource 工具) · 用法

**id**　9801b82e9458117cbc-3

**english**　Batch Import Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- AllSource 工具箱
- 轉換工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/intelligence/batch-import-data.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/intelligence/batch-import-data.html

**text**　運行此工具時，座標系具有以下注意事項： 對于 shapefile、.kml、.kmz、.geojson 和 .gpx 文件，輸出資料集與輸入資料集的座標系相同。 KML 和 GPX 規范要求輸入資料集的座標系為 GCS WGS1984。 對于 Excel 工作簿和表格文本文件，輸出座標系將為 GCS WGS1984。
如果圖徵的任何輸入座標無效（空值或無效座標記法），則相應圖徵將具有空幾何。 檢查幾何工具可用于識別輸出中的空幾何。
表格文本文件（.csv、.txt 和 .tab）以及 Excel 工作表支持的座標為緯度和經度（十進制度）、美國軍事格網參考系 (MGRS) 和美國國家格網 (USNG)。 在源中按以下順序搜索座標列：單獨列中的緯度和經度、單個列中的緯度和經度、MGRS，最后是 USNG。 注: 對于單個列中的緯度和經度值，緯度值必須在前，隨后是以空格、逗號或斜線分隔的經度。 有效列名稱如下： Latitude—latitude、lat、y_coordinate、y_coord 和 y Longitude—longitude、long、lon、x_coordinate、x_coord 和 x Latitude/longitude—latitude_longitude、lat_long、lat_lon、xy_coordinate、xy_coordinates、xy_coord、xy_coords 和 xy MGRS coordinate—mgrs、mgrs_coordinate、mgrs_coordinates、mgrs_coord 和 mgrs_coords USNG coordinate—usng、usng_coordinate、usng_coordinates、usng_coord 和 usng_coords
將導入 Excel 工作簿中的所有工作表。
將根據如下源資料文件中的符號系統向添加到地圖中的輸出圖層應用相應的符號系統： 注: 在工具執行時，ArcGIS Pro 工程中必須存在活動地圖，以保留符號系統。 如果工具在 ArcGIS Pro 工程之外運行（即，通過獨立 Python 腳本運行），則不會保留符號系統。 對于從 .kml 或 .kmz 文件創建的輸出圖層，將從源 .kml 或 .kmz 文件中提取符號系統并將其應用于輸出圖層。 可以在與源 .kml 或 .kmz 文件相同的文件夾中提供一個圖層文件（.lyrx 或 .lyr）以覆蓋此符號系統。 圖層文件與源資料文件必須具有相同的基本名稱，并在名稱后追加下劃線 (_) 和要覆蓋圖層的幾何類型（點、折線或面）。 例如，如果 .kml 文件命名為 World Airports.kml 且包含點、線和面圖徵，并且您希望覆蓋所有幾何類型的符號系統，則圖層文件將命名為 World Airports_Points.lyrx、World Airports_Polylines.lyrx 和 World Airports_Polygons.lyrx。 幾何類型值區分大小寫。 如果給定的幾何類型不存在圖層文件，則會將源 .kml 或 .kmz 文件中的符號系統應用到輸出圖層。 For output layers created from sources other than .kml 或 .kmz 文件，如果在與源資料文件相同的文件夾中存在圖層文件（.lyrx 或 .lyr），則會將符號系統應用到輸出圖層。 圖層文件與源資料文件必須具有相同的基本名稱（Excel 工作表和 .geojson 文件除外）。 對于 Excel 工作表，圖層文件的名稱必須與源資料文件的基本名稱相同，且名稱后追加有下劃線 () 和工作表名稱。 例如，如果 Excel 文件命名為 World.xlsx 且包含名為 Cities (2018) 的工作表，則圖層文件必須命名為 World_Cities (2018).lyrx 或 World_Cities (2018).lyr。 對于 .geojson 文件，圖層文件的名稱必須與源資料文件的基本名稱相同，且名稱后追加有下劃線 () 和輸出幾何類型（點、多點、折線和面）。 如果圖層文件不存在，則會對輸出圖層應用默認符號。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/intelligence/batch-import-data.html#usage

## 批量導入資料 (AllSource 工具) · 參數

**id**　9801b82e9458117cbc-7

**english**　Batch Import Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- AllSource 工具箱
- 轉換工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/intelligence/batch-import-data.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/intelligence/batch-import-data.html

**parameter**　filter

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 過濾器 （可選）
- 應用過濾器以限制導入文件夾的文件。 過濾器的以下通配符適用于輸入資料的完整路徑： *—匹配任何字符 ?—匹配單個字符 [range]—匹配范圍內的單個字符 [!range]— 匹配不在范圍內的任意字符 可以通過使用豎線或管道分隔符 (|) 將每個模式分隔開來，從而將多個模式添加到過濾器。 模式比較不區分大小寫，因此，例如如果使用 *airport.shp、*AIRPORT.SHP 或 *Airport.shp 模式，將導入相同的 shapefile。 以下是過濾器示例： 要導入所有 shapefile，請使用 *.shp。 要導入所有 shapefile 和所有 .kml 文件，請使用 *.shp|*.kml。 要導入文件路徑或文件名中包含 airport 的所有文件，請使用 *airport*。 要導入文件路徑或文件名中包含 airport 的所有 .geojson 文件，請使用 *airport*.geojson。 要導入名稱中包含 airport 且其后追加有任意兩個字符的所有 .kmz 文件，請使用 *airport??.kmz。 要導入文件路徑或文件名中包含 1990 到 1997 的所有文件，請使用 *199[0-7]*。 要導入文件路徑中包含確切文件夾名稱 airfacilities 的所有 shapefile，請使用 *\airfacilities\*.shp。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/intelligence/batch-import-data.html#parameters
